Method: 提出平衡多模态标签重塑（BMLR），从标签侧设计出发，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态特征空间与共享标签空间之间的映射难度，从而促进模态交互并向各模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的大量实验表明，BMLR能够持续提升多模态性能，并与多样的模型设计展现出强兼容性。

Conclusion: BMLR是首个从标签侧设计促进多模态平衡的方法，通过均衡跨模态映射难度有效缓解模态不平衡问题，且具有良好的通用性和兼容性。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [11] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏置和噪声的三部分统计框架研究柏拉图表征假说(PRH)，提出柏拉图对齐源于物体与属性间的普遍关系（由线性表征假说LRH编码），并发现稀疏自编码器提取的线性特征能增强跨模态对齐；通过中心化和归一化可部分缓解模型偏置差异；有限样本训练导致的表征噪声与数据稀缺性正相关（词频与对齐度正相关）。最终提出一个统计模型完善LRH并解释现代AI架构中的对齐现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究柏拉图表征假说(PRH)的内在机制，即为什么不同AI模型会趋向于学习相似的表征空间，以及在信号、偏置和噪声三个维度下这一现象如何产生和变化。

Method: 提出信号-偏置-噪声三部分统计框架：1）信号方面，通过稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征，验证线性表征假说(LRH)对PRH的解释力；2）偏置方面，采用中心化和归一化方法改善跨模型对齐；3）噪声方面，分析词频与对齐度的相关性以揭示数据稀缺性对表征噪声的影响。

Result: 1）稀疏表征比稠密表征展现出更强的跨模态对齐能力；2）中心化和归一化能一致地改善跨模型对齐；3）词频与对齐度之间存在强且一致的正相关关系，表明表征噪声由数据稀缺性驱动。

Conclusion: 通过信号、偏置和噪声的综合分析，提出了一个完善线性表征假说的统计模型，能够解释现代多样AI架构中表征对齐的进一步现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [12] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向量化基准测试的保守最小可检测效应（MDE）界限，将配对二元样本量计算方法适配到量化场景，并通过无配对预注册审计验证：在n=100子样本中，大部分方差来自二项抽样噪声而非基准不可靠性；同时MMLU提示模板变异性（2-10 pp）超过量化差异（最大3.2 pp），提示量化审计必须先固定提示模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试中，研究者常报告FP16与低精度（如NF4）之间的性能差异，但缺乏评估该差异是否超越随机噪声的统计工具。现有基准测试的方差往往被归因于“基准不可靠性”，而未区分二项抽样噪声。本文旨在提供一个简单、保守的统计界限，将“我的量化声称有多可靠？”转化为基准设计者可在实验前承诺的预算公式。

Method: 将Miettinen（1968）经典配对二元样本量计算方法适配到量化基准测试场景，推导出保守的最小可检测效应（MDE）界限δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)，其中m为配对样本数，ρ_d为FP16与NF4之间的不一致率。在4个模型×4个基准（k=5次分割，n=100）上演示该界限，并附加MMLU提示模板变异性研究以对比量化噪声与提示噪声尺度。

Result: 假设未测量的ρ_d=0.10，所有NF4-FP16差异均低于MDE；大多数跨分割标准差在±1.5个百分点内接近二项参考√(p(1-p)/n)。OPT-WinoGrande单元格在ρ_d=0.10时低于MDE，但在ρ_d=0.05时高于MDE，体现了规划权衡。MMLU提示模板变异性（2-10 pp）等于或超过量化最大差异（3.2 pp）。

Conclusion: n=100子样本中报告的“基准不可靠性”大部分实为二项抽样噪声；量化审计必须先固定提示模板，否则模板方差会混入噪声基底；提供了可操作的五行预注册模板，帮助研究者预先设定量化声称可信度的统计标准。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [13] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将上下文蒸馏重新定义为潜在记忆管理问题，通过将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器构建模块化记忆库，利用自门控机制决定是否激活潜在记忆，并通过缓存共享提高推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了在非理想环境下多个蒸馏潜在记忆应如何存储、检索和安全激活的问题。

Method: 1) 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器，形成模块化记忆库；2) 根据查询检索候选记忆，路由到最合适的适配器；3) 使用自门控(Self-Gating)机制决定是否激活潜在记忆；4) 引入缓存共享减少推理管理开销。

Result: 该方法显著优于带检索的基线方法，自门控通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提高了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏形式化为潜在记忆管理问题，通过模块化记忆库+显式记忆选择+自门控机制，有效提升了多记忆场景下的检索准确性和激活鲁棒性。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [14] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析LoRA微调对大型语言模型内部表征的影响，发现LoRA诱导的特征与预训练SAE字典之间存在较弱的几何对齐，且LoRA更新在残差流中占据部分独特的表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调虽被广泛使用，但其对模型内部表征变化的影响尚不清晰，缺乏对其几何结构的深入理解。

Method: 提出delta激活框架隔离LoRA适配器贡献，使用Gemma-2-9B模型在rank 4/8/16/32下训练适配器特异性SAE，通过余弦相似度、主成分分析和CKA评估表征对齐。

Result: LoRA诱导的特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间几何对齐较弱；适配器特异性SAE能更有效地重建delta激活；特征密度随rank和深度增加，而几何差异在不同rank间相对稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调会诱导出预训练可解释性词典未能完全捕捉的特征结构，对机械可解释性、适应性分析和微调模型的安全审计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [15] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的固有几何结构控制扩散模型，通过条件期望算子的奇异函数学习低维表征，实现无需重训练或反向传播的高保真控制，在CIFAR-10上条件准确率提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍，并揭示了生成过程中的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型控制方法需要重训练或使用denoiser反向传播，计算成本高且不稳定。作者希望利用生成过程的几何结构，找到少量信息丰富的特征来实现高效、稳定的控制。

Method: 将噪声数据中信息丰富的特征表征为条件期望算子的奇异函数，通过自监督目标学习该基函数，然后将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）投影到该基上，直接作用于采样轨迹。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上条件准确率比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍；支持标签、CLIP和空间掩码等多种引导方式，无需辅助模型；揭示了生成过程中的相变，确定了最优引导时间窗口。

Conclusion: Spectral Guidance提供了一种通用、高效、稳定的扩散模型控制框架，无需重训练即可实现多种形式的引导，同时为理解生成过程的动态特性提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [16] [Sequential Physics-Constrained Neural Operator Forward Modeling for the $\textit{Norne}$ Reservoir System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28909)
*Clement Etienam,Juntao Yang,Oleg Ovcharenko,Nick Luiken,Tsubasa Onishi,Nefeli Moridis,Issam Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于神经算子（特别是FNO和PINO）的三相黑油油藏动态序贯替代建模框架，在Norne基准油藏（113,344个网格单元，30个时间步，3298天）上实现了理论分析和实验验证。主要贡献包括：乘积Sobolev空间中的泛函分析公式、Wasserstein-2距离的协变量偏移量化、物理约束谱稳定性分析以及K步TBPTT梯度分析。实验表明，自回归PINO替代模型在3298天范围内保持高精度（油R²>0.99，气R²>0.90，压力R²≈0.80），在8块NVIDIA B200 GPU上训练不到1小时，1000成员集成在单块B200 GPU上不到1分钟，相比OPM有限体积模拟器实现约10⁴倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 油藏数值模拟中，传统有限体积模拟器（如OPM）计算成本极高，尤其在进行不确定性量化、历史匹配和优化等需要大量模拟的任务时。因此，需要开发高效且准确的替代模型来加速油藏动态预测。

Method: 本文构建了基于傅里叶神经算子（FNO）及其物理信息变体（PINO）的序贯替代建模框架。理论部分围绕四个问题展开：泛函分析公式（乘积Sobolev空间中的隐式时间步映射适定性和Lipschitz估计）、协变量偏移量化（Wasserstein-2距离界）、物理约束谱稳定性（PINO训练降低学习Jacobian谱半径）、K步TBPTT梯度分析（几何偏差衰减和Adam收敛速度）。实验在Norne基准油藏的46×112×22网格（113,344个单元）上，使用8块NVIDIA B200 GPU进行训练和评估。

Result: 自回归PINO替代模型在3298天预测中保持高精度：油相R²>0.99，气相R²>0.90，压力R²≈0.80，水相R²单调提升。1000成员集成在单块B200 GPU上不到1分钟完成，相比OPM有限体积模拟器实现约10⁴倍壁钟时间加速。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实验两方面证明了基于神经算子的替代建模方法在复杂油藏动态预测中的有效性和高效性，为大规模油藏模拟中的不确定性量化和快速预测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive mathematical and computational framework for sequential surrogate modeling of three-phase black-oil reservoir dynamics using neural operators, with particular emphasis on Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) and their physics-informed variant (PINO). The application focus is the Norne benchmark reservoir, defined on a heterogeneous $46\times112\times22$ grid ($N=113,344$ cells), with a production history spanning $T=30$ timesteps covering 3298 days. Our theoretical contributions are organized around four interlocking problems: (1) functional-analytic formulation in a product-Sobolev-space setting, including well-posedness of the implicit timestep map and sharp local Lipschitz estimates; (2) covariate shift quantification, proving that the Wasserstein-2 distance grows as $W_2 \leq \varepsilon(L^n-1)/(L-1)$, with exponential population-risk discrepancy for $L>1$; (3) physics-constrained spectral stability, showing PINO training with $λ_R \geq λ^*_R$ reduces the learned Jacobian spectral radius to $ρ_F + Cλ_R^{-1/2}$, yielding uniform-in-time rollout error $|δ_n| \leq \varepsilon/(1-ρ)$; and (4) $K$-step TBPTT gradient analysis, deriving geometric bias decay $O(ρ^K)$, optimal window $K^ = O(\log(T/σ^2))$, and Adam convergence $O(1/\sqrt{t}) + O(ρ^{K^*})$. Empirical validation confirms all theoretical predictions: autoregressive PINO surrogates sustain $R^2>0.99$ (oil), $R^2>0.90$ (gas), $R^2\approx 0.80$ (pressure), and monotonically improving $R^2$ (water) across the full 3298-day horizon, trained on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs in under one hour. A 1000-member ensemble runs in under one minute on a single B200 GPU, giving a ${\sim}10^4\times$ wall-clock speedup over the OPM finite-volume simulator.

</details>


### [17] [Cycle-Space Informed Detection of Autoencoded Blind False Data Injection Attacks on Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28912)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑感知的环空间检测器（CSD），利用电网的Cycle-Space约束来检测数据驱动的虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），克服了自编码器等数据驱动方法因利用Jacobian零空间而规避传统残差检测器的缺陷，并在多个IEEE标准系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法多依赖测量数据的统计或数据驱动分析，但攻击者可利用相同的数据结构构造隐秘扰动（如自编码器学习测量流形并沿Jacobian零空间生成扰动），使得残差型坏数据检测器和时序异常检测器均失效，亟需一种不受数据驱动攻击利用零空间能力影响的检测方法。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的环空间检测器（Cycle-Space Detector, CSD），利用电网拓扑的Cycle-Space施加结构约束以增强零空间估计；采用最小环基（Minimum Cycle Basis, MCB）实现最优泛化误差；方法不依赖精确线路参数，仅利用拓扑衍生的环约束。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、57、118节点系统上的仿真结果表明，所提CSD方法能够在实际测量噪声下有效检测数据驱动的FDIA。

Conclusion: 基于拓扑Cycle-Space的检测器通过结构约束取代纯数值零空间估计，提升了正常测量与受攻击测量之间的可分性，无需精确线路参数即可有效检测利用零空间的数据驱动FDIA。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI-driven data centers and large-scale energy storage systems is increasing the reliance of power system operation on real-time measurement data and automated decision-making. However, many existing detection methods rely on statistical or data-driven analysis of measurements and can fail when attackers exploit the same data structure to craft stealthy perturbations. To illustrate this limitation, we demonstrate a blind False Data Injection Attack (FDIA) in which an Autoencoder learns the measurement manifold and generates perturbations aligned with the Jacobian null space, thereby allowing the attack to evade both residual-based baddata detectors and time-series anomaly detectors. To mitigate data-driven FDIAs which exploit the null space, we propose a topology-informed Cycle-Space Detector (CSD) that leverages the Cycle-Space of the network to impose structural constraints that enhance null space estimation. In addition, we prove that by using the Minimum Cycle Basis (MCB), the proposed CSD achieves the optimal generalization error for attack detection. By exploiting topology-derived cycle constraints rather than relying solely on numerical null space estimation, the proposed method does not require precise line parameters and improves the separation between normal and attacked measurements. Simulation results on IEEE 14-, 30-, 57-, and 118-bus systems demonstrate that the proposed method effectively detects data-driven FDIAs under realistic measurement noise.

</details>


### [18] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于LLM的奖励塑形在稀疏结构化强化学习任务中的应用，提出将单次生成范式重新定义为调试过程。通过PPO训练与MiniGrid/MuJoCo评估，识别出两种主要失败模式（奖励洪泛、语义/API误解）及弱塑形问题，并引入诊断驱动的迭代精炼方法。实验表明该方法在DoorKey-8x8（2.3%→97.6%）和KeyCorridor（31.2%→86.7%）上取得显著提升，但存在高种子方差和连续控制任务的边界限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM奖励塑形方法采用单次生成（one-shot）范式，但面对稀疏结构化任务时失败率高且缺乏系统性的调试机制。作者旨在论证该问题更适合被框架化为"调试"而非"生成"，并通过系统化的失败模式分类与诊断驱动的迭代精炼来提升奖励函数质量。

Method: 提出诊断驱动的迭代精炼框架：(1) 对LLM生成的奖励函数进行审计，识别两种主要失败模式（奖励洪泛和语义/API误解）及弱塑形问题；(2) 利用训练诊断指标和失败模式分类学指导目标性的奖励函数修订；(3) 设置多组对照实验：指标重提示、静态词汇控制、预算匹配、Best-of-3消融、组件移除测试和敏感性分析。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%；静态词汇控制恢复大部分差距（87.6%; 70.7%），表明分类学提示是主要机制；连续控制任务中基于成功率的诊断在密集奖励场景下失效；交叉方差设计显示LLM奖励函数方差主导时增益更大，但bootstrap区间宽。

Conclusion: 诊断驱动的迭代精炼框架适用于PPO下的稀疏结构化任务及可靠接口场景；分类学提示是核心机制，动态标签仅提供部分增量证据；方法存在边界限制（连续控制任务中可能失灵），且事件文本等字段效果不确定。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [19] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CosmicFish-HRM 是一种紧凑型语言模型，通过分层推理模块（HRM）动态分配推理深度，在不显著增加参数量的情况下实现自适应推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备强推理能力，但往往以大量参数和昂贵推理成本为代价。本文探索在紧凑模型中通过自适应推理深度来替代单纯依靠参数规模提升推理能力的方向。

Method: 提出 CosmicFish-HRM 模型，核心是分层推理模块（HRM），通过高低层推理循环动态调整计算量，学习根据输入复杂度决定何时停止推理。结合 Grouped Query Attention、RoPE、SwiGLU 等现代 Transformer 组件。

Result: 模型学习到非均匀的推理行为，能根据不同任务和输入分配不同的推理步数。小规模下推理模块引入额外开销，但推测随模型规模增大该开销占比会降低。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可能成为替代单纯依赖参数规模以提升推理能力的有前景方向。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [20] [Conf-Gen: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28920)
*Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem,Kevin Zhang,Wei Cui,Marc T. Law,Kin Kwan Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Generation (Conf-Gen)框架，将共形预测和共形风险控制扩展到无监督生成式AI任务（如大语言模型、图像生成器等），在放宽理论假设的同时统一并推广了先前尝试，并在图像去记忆、对话系统和AI智能体等新领域展示了应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共形预测（CP）和共形风险控制（CRC）框架主要适用于监督式机器学习，无法直接应用于当前由无监督生成式模型（如LLM、图像生成器）驱动的AI突破，因此需要一种新的理论框架来为这些生成式任务提供正式的不确定性量化保证。

Method: Conf-Gen通过适配CRC框架并放宽其理论假设，将共形方法论扩展到生成式任务中，统一并泛化了先前将CP应用于LLM的尝试。

Result: Conf-Gen被成功应用于三个新领域：保证图像生成器不产生记忆性图像、确保对话AI系统提出足够多的澄清问题、以及验证AI智能体输出的正确性。

Conclusion: Conf-Gen为生成式AI模型提供了正式的不确定性量化保证框架，拓展了共形方法的应用边界，在解决生成式AI的安全性和可靠性问题方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) and its extension, conformal risk control (CRC), are established frameworks for quantifying uncertainty in supervised machine learning through formal guarantees. However, recent breakthroughs in artificial intelligence (AI) have been driven by unsupervised generative models, such as large language models (LLMs) and image generators, which are not directly compatible with CP or CRC. In this work we introduce conformal generation (Conf-Gen), a general framework adapting CRC to generative tasks while relaxing its theoretical assumptions. Conf-Gen unifies and generalizes previous attempts to apply CP to LLMs, and extends conformal methodology to entirely new domains. We demonstrate the flexibility of Conf-Gen through some novel applications, including obtaining conformal guarantees on: image generators producing non-memorized images, conversational AI systems having asked enough clarifying questions, and the output of AI agents being correct.

</details>


### [21] [A Training-Time Diagnostic for Generalization via the Log-Alignment Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28975)
*Ali Shehper,Ashish Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了log对齐比（LAR）为权重谱与激活谱之间的重叠度量，并证明在grokking和大型语言模型预训练中，LAR能够有效追踪记忆与泛化之间的转换、预测学习函数的有效维度以及监测泛化差距，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以从参数-激活对齐的角度解释模型从记忆到泛化的转变，且缺乏一种计算高效、无需验证数据的指标来监测过拟合。

Method: 将LAR重新表述为权重谱p（矩阵归一化平方奇异值的分布）与激活谱q（输入在奇异方向上的归一化平方投影分布）之间的重叠度量。在grokking和3B参数语言模型预训练两个场景中分析LAR的动态变化。

Result: 在grokking中，LAR可预测学习函数的有效维度k ≈ n^{2(1-LAR)}；在3B参数语言模型预训练中，LAR偏离无过拟合基线的程度跟踪泛化差距，其下降速率在过拟合逼近时增加。

Conclusion: LAR是无需验证数据、计算开销极小的有效泛化监测指标，能够统一刻画不同场景下的记忆-泛化转换。

Abstract: We study the log-alignment ratio (LAR), a measure of parameter-activation alignment, introduced in parameterization theory. We reformulate it as the overlap between a weight spectrum $p$ of the normalized squared singular values of a matrix and an activation spectrum $q$ of the normalized squared projections of inputs onto its singular directions. We show that unembedding LAR tracks the transition between memorization and generalization in two different settings by capturing the spread of $p$ and $q$ during training. In grokking, LAR predicts the effective dimension of the learned function: $k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$, where $n$ is the input dimension of the matrix. In 3B-parameter language model pre-training, its deviation from a non-overfitting baseline tracks the generalization gap, and its rate of decline increases as overfitting approaches. LAR is computable from quantities available during the forward pass with negligible computational overhead, and requires no held-out validation data.

</details>


### [22] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究采用多种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）分析用于EEG-based抑郁症检测的InceptionTime模型，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）产生部分收敛的归因模式，但方法间存在显著差异，强调事后可解释性在精神疾病黑箱EEG分类器中的用途与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在基于EEG的抑郁症检测中表现优异，但其决策过程难以解释，缺乏对模型可解释性的系统研究。

Method: 采用InceptionTime架构进行EEG-based MDD分类，应用五种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance），在subject-level分层5折交叉验证框架下进行全局归因聚合分析。

Result: 不同可解释性方法揭示部分收敛的归因模式，主要集中在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）；梯度和基于扰动的方间一致性较高，DeepSHAP产生明显不同的归因分布；方法间变异性反映了方法学假设对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 不同事后可解释性方法捕捉到EEG深度学习模型中部分重叠的相关性结构，归因模式与既往EEG抑郁症研究基本一致，但应视为探索性分析而非确切的神经生理生物标志物或临床适用证据。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [23] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSimSiam是一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，仅使用正样本对即可从fMRI数据中学习鲁棒且泛化的特征，在多个下游任务中优于全监督基线，性能接近大规模模型，特别适合数据有限的神经影像应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据获取成本高、样本量小、标注质量不稳定，加上数据高维度特性，导致模型过拟合风险大。现有fMRI基础模型预训练和微调的计算资源需求过高。

Method: 提出BrainSimSiam框架，采用轻量级自监督学习，仅使用正样本对（positive-only data pairs）进行训练，无需负样本或标签信息，学习鲁棒且可泛化的fMRI表示。

Result: 所学表示在多种下游分类和回归任务中表现优异，超过了全监督基线方法，性能接近大规模预训练模型。

Conclusion: BrainSimSiam为数据受限的神经影像应用提供了一种高效、轻量的自监督学习方案，证明了轻量级自监督方法在fMRI表示学习中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [24] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRe是一种无需训练的推理时即插即用方法，通过动态评估每步中高冲突/高不确定性的交互子集（而非全量或静态稀疏化），显著提升扩散组合优化求解器的可扩展性。在MIS、TSP等任务上实现8-15倍加速，12-44倍内存压缩，且保持解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器在推理时需要反复评估稠密的边/因子交互，导致推理耗时长、内存瓶颈严重（尤其在规模扩大时），传统静态稀疏化（如固定kNN图）无法自适应调整计算分配。

Method: 受多体物理计算方法启发，LoRe在扩散过程的每一步中，只评估固定比例的交互子集。通过动态路由计算资源到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上，而非使用固定稀疏化策略（如静态kNN图或静态掩码），实现计算预算的自适应分配。

Result: 在最大独立集（MIS）问题上，可扩展性提升3倍以上，约8倍加速和约12倍峰值内存压缩，解质量保持不变。在大规模旅行商问题（TSP）上，n=1000时约15倍加速和44倍内存压缩，竞争性的路径质量；对拓扑结构变化具有零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRe作为一种无需训练、推理时即插即用的方法，通过动态预算分配有效解决了扩散组合优化求解器的可扩展性问题，在多个任务上实现了显著的加速和内存压缩，同时保持解质量，展示了跨任务泛化能力和拓扑鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>


### [25] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST是一种因果智能方法，通过学习上下文特定的因果图与结构因果模型，结合约束感知的多目标优化，在硅中设计受约束的干预策略以实现用户定义的状态转换，并在合成与真实生物数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型大多缺乏机制性洞察，并且没有为决策提供原则性的框架，难以通过定向干预将系统从一种状态驱动到另一种状态。

Method: COAST包含四个模块：特征选择、因果发现、因果建模、干预识别与评估。它学习上下文特定的因果图和结构因果模型，将分布偏移归因于机制层面的因果驱动因素，并采用约束感知的多目标优化来平衡转换效能、干预复杂度和目标状态稳定性。

Result: 在合成基准和真实生物数据集上，COAST恢复了关键因果驱动因素，并识别出稳健的单目标和多目标干预策略，实现了期望的状态转换，同时提供了透明的机制性解释以指导实验验证。

Conclusion: COAST提供了一种模块化、领域无关的因果智能框架，能够为状态转换问题设计受约束的干预策略，兼具可解释性和实验可验证性。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [26] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨模型熵（CME）作为无标签奖励信号，用独立验证器对生成器响应的平均对数似然替代自参考信号，无需真实奖励或人工标注即可进行强化学习后训练，在开放指令跟随任务上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练依赖真实可验证奖励（局限于数学、代码等自动可验证领域）或昂贵且易被奖励欺骗的人工偏好标签；无标签方法使用自参考信号（如多数投票、token熵）但会强化模型自身错误。

Method: 提出Cross-Model Entropy (CME)，即生成器输出在独立验证器模型下的平均对数似然，作为无标签奖励信号；将其集成到GRPO训练循环中，不改变其他训练流程。

Result: 在UltraFeedback提示下的开放指令跟随任务（AlpacaEval 2.0评估）中，CME奖励在四个模型族（Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo）和三种训练阶段（预训练、SFT、指令微调）的头对头LLM评判比较中均击败未训练基线，调整后的胜率范围为52.5%至71.4%。

Conclusion: CME作为连续、免训练、基于独立验证器的无标签奖励信号，可有效扩展强化学习后训练到开放指令跟随等自参考信号不适用的领域，且不易被利用。

Abstract: Post-training large language models with reinforcement learning is bottlenecked by the reward signal. Existing approaches require either ground-truth verifiable rewards, restricting training to domains with automatic correctness checks (e.g., mathematics, code execution), or human preference labels, which are expensive to collect and prone to reward hacking. Recent label-free methods replace ground-truth verifiers with self-referential signals like majority voting or token entropy over a model's own outputs, but risk reinforcing a model's own errors. In this work we propose Cross-Model Entropy (CME), the mean log-likelihood of a generator's response under a separate verifier model, as a label-free reward signal for RL post-training. CME is continuous, training-free, and grounded in the principle that responses a verifier finds unsurprising are likely correct or high quality. Because the verifier is independent of the generator, the signal cannot be gamed through self-consistency. We integrate CME into GRPO with no other changes to the training loop, extending label-free RL to open-ended instruction following -- a regime where self-referential signals are inapplicable or poorly suited. On open-ended instruction following (UltraFeedback prompts, evaluated on AlpacaEval 2.0), CME rewards beat the untrained base in head-to-head LLM-as-Judge comparisons across four model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo) and three training regimes (pretrained, SFT, and instruction-tuned), with tie-adjusted win rates ranging from 52.5% to 71.4%. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [27] [Theoretical Foundations and Effective Algorithms for Policy-Aware Simulator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29032)
*Christoph Dann,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将模型学习目标从预测准确性转向战略鲁棒性，通过零和极小极大博弈框架，在连续控制任务中实现纯仿真训练策略匹配近最优真实世界性能，关键区域预测误差降低1.5-2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习智能体通常通过最小化预测损失来学习世界模型，但强大的RL优化器会不可避免地利用微小的模型不准确之处，导致模拟器利用和现实差距——策略在仿真中成功但在真实世界中失败。因此需要为学习模拟器设计新的目标函数。

Method: 将模拟器学习目标形式化为零和极小极大博弈，包含模型玩家和对抗策略玩家；提出基于评论家的简化方法将全局策略价值差距约束为局部评论家损失；推导Error-MDP对偶性，将最坏情况策略寻找转化为标准RL问题（奖励为一步评论家误差），从而得到可证明收敛的主动数据选择算法。

Result: 在连续控制任务上，关键战略区域的预测误差降低1.5-2.2倍，纯仿真训练的策略能够匹配近最优的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: 战略鲁棒性作为模拟器学习目标能够有效弥合仿真与现实之间的差距，理论上的在线学习保证、评论家简化边界和Error-MDP对偶性为该方法提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) agents typically learn world models by minimizing predictive loss. However, powerful RL optimizers inevitably exploit minor model inaccuracies, leading to simulator exploitation and a reality gap where policies succeed in simulation but fail in the real world. We propose that the objective for learning simulators should be strategic robustness rather than predictive accuracy, and formulate this as a zero-sum minimax game between a model player and an adversarial policy player. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis: (1) an online learning guarantee showing the game is learnable with sublinear regret bounds; (2) a tractable critic-based simplification bounding the global policy-value gap by the local critic's loss; and (3) an Error-MDP duality, proving that finding the worst-case policy is formally dual to a standard RL problem where the reward is the one-step critic error. This duality yields a provably convergent active data selection algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate that our approach reduces prediction error in strategically important regions by $1.5$-$2.2\times$ and enables policies trained purely in simulation to match near-optimal real-world performance.

</details>


### [28] [Moment Matching Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29033)
*Yiyan,Liang,Sifei Liu,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL)框架，利用最大均值差异（MMD）技术匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，加速基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中的推理过程，在D4RL任务中实现了更高的计算效率，并在离线到在线RL任务中表现出更快的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数和流的生成模型虽能捕获复杂分布但在RL中因迭代采样导致推理延迟过长，成为计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出MoMa QL框架，利用统计假设检验中的最大均值差异（MMD）技术，匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，对条件得分函数施加强正则化以实现分布级收敛。

Result: 在D4RL任务中计算效率更高且性能相当或更具竞争力；在离线到在线RL任务中，因加速了基于流的策略的动作采样过程，展现出更优的在线交互微调适应能力。

Conclusion: MoMa QL通过矩匹配机制有效解决了生成模型在RL中的推理延迟问题，在多种超参数下保持稳定，并能加速在线适应过程。

Abstract: Score-based and flow-based generative models exhibit remarkable expressive capacity in capturing complex distributions, and have been extensively deployed in tasks ranging from image generation to reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, these models suffer from prolonged inference latency, which imposes a significant computational bottleneck in RL with iterative sampling. To overcome this limitation, we propose a new framework named Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL), which utilizes a technique from statistical hypothesis testing known as maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) that intend to match all orders of statistics between the original and target distribution. By enforcing strong regularization on all moment statistics, this algorithm guarantees distribution-level convergence for conditional score function and remains stable under various hyperparameters. Empirically, we show that our method MoMa QL is more computationally efficient with a comparable if not competitive performance in various D4RL tasks. Remarkably, by accelerating the action sampling process for flow-based policies, MoMa QL demonstrates superior performance in offline-to-online RL tasks because of faster and stronger adaptability for online interactive finetuning.

</details>


### [29] [Knowledge Offloading: Decomposing LLMs into Sparse Backbones and Memory Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29075)
*Karim Galliamov,Rochelle Choenni,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出知识卸载(KOFF)框架，将预训练LLM分解为稀疏共享主干和领域特定记忆模块。通过结构化剪枝掩码、LoRA适配器和可学习KV缓存，在约12%全局稀疏度下保持模型性能，且语言特定神经元被优先移除，语言通用神经元保留在主干中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否将LLM的通用能力和领域知识重新组织：通用计算保留在共享主干中，专门知识迁移到外部记忆模块。

Method: 基于冻结基模型，联合学习结构化剪枝掩码和轻量恢复模块（LoRA适配器+可学习键值缓存），实现知识卸载。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen（3B-8B）模型上，约12%全局稀疏度下KOFF保持大部分未剪枝模型性能，而直接剪枝性能急剧下降。LoRA和KV记忆互补，语言特定神经元被优先移除。

Conclusion: 知识可以在共享核心和可交换外部记忆之间重新分配，验证了分解预训练LLM的可行性。

Abstract: LLMs encode both general capabilities and domain-specific knowledge in a single set of parameters. We ask whether this capacity can be reorganized: keeping broadly useful computation in a shared backbone, while moving specialized knowledge into external memory modules. We propose \emph{knowledge offloading} (KOFF), a framework for decomposing a pretrained LLM into a sparse shared backbone and domain-specific memories. Starting from a frozen base model, we jointly learn a structured pruning mask and lightweight recovery modules, implemented as LoRA adapters and learned key-value caches. Across Llama and Qwen models from 3B to 8B, we find that non-trivial capacity can be moved out of the shared backbone without a large loss in model ability. At around 12\% global sparsity, KOFF preserves much of the unpruned model's performance, while pruning the same frozen model without memories degrades sharply. Ablations show that LoRA and learned KV memories are complementary, and specialization analyses suggest that the learned decomposition is meaningful: language-specific neurons are preferentially removed while language-general neurons largely remain in the backbone. These results suggest that knowledge can be reallocated between a shared core and swappable external memories.

</details>


### [30] [OISD: On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29089)
*Xinyu Liu,Darryl Cherian Jacob,Yang Zhou,Jindong Wang,Pan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OISD框架，通过在策略内部自蒸馏（将最终层的预测信号迁移到中间层），利用logit对齐和注意力对齐两种机制提升推理能力，在数学推理任务上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法主要使用稀疏的最终结果奖励来优化输出策略，忽略了中间表示中编码的预测信号，导致中间层未能有效参与推理过程。

Method: OISD框架在GRPO优化过程中，将最终层同时作为策略和分离的内部教师，通过两种机制引导选定的中间层：1) logit对齐——迁移高层推理行为（如何思考）；2) 注意力对齐——强制执行一致的注意力模式（关注哪里）。使用带符号优势加权的Jensen-Shannon对齐来蒸馏信息性中间表示，同时保持策略一致性。

Result: 在四个数学推理任务上，OISD相比强推理RL基线取得了显著且一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过在策略内部自蒸馏的方式将最终层的预测信号迁移到中间层，能够有效提升推理能力，且无需外部特权信息。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning (RL) post-training approaches primarily optimize the final output policy using sparse outcome-level rewards, while largely overlooking predictive signals encoded in intermediate representations. In this paper, we introduce a new paradigm called on-policy internal self-distillation and propose the OISD framework, which improves reasoning by transferring on-policy predictive signals from the final layer to intermediate representations. During rollout and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) optimization, the final layer acts as both the policy and a detached internal teacher for selected intermediate layers, which are guided to align with it through two complementary mechanisms: logit alignment, which transfers high-level reasoning behaviors (how to think), and attention alignment, which enforces consistent attention patterns (where to look) from the final layer to the selected intermediate layer, both without requiring external privileged information. Our OISD, together with GRPO, employs signed advantage-weighted Jensen--Shannon alignment to distill informative intermediate representations while preserving policy consistency under a unified acting policy. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of OISD, with substantial and consistent improvements over strong reasoning RL baselines across four mathematical reasoning tasks. The code will be released at https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/OISD

</details>


### [31] [Model Merging by Output-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29101)
*Bethan Evans,Benjamin Etheridge,Stephen Roberts,Jared Tanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将模型合并形式化为一个关于残差更新的凸二次规划（QP）问题，通过校准输入和微调模型输出来最小化平方输出校准目标，统一了现有方法，并提供了可预测合并质量的理论诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如task arithmetic、model soups、TIES、DARE）虽然计算高效且经验成功，但依赖于启发式设计选择，缺乏形式化的最优性保证，迫切需要理论框架来指导合并过程。

Method: 将模型合并建模为关于残差更新的凸二次规划问题，通过校准输入和微调模型输出来优化平方输出校准目标；提出封闭形式的诊断指标（残差能量被选定基捕获的比例）来预测合并质量；扩展到多层合并的逐层顺序算法。

Result: 在单层设置中，QP方法匹配或超越现有方法；最优基相比低成本的基对角线QP带来显著提升；在语言和视觉基准上，多层逐层合并方法持续取得更好的效果。

Conclusion: 凸二次规划框架为模型合并提供了理论基础和最优性保证，同时统一了现有启发式方法，并在实践中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model without retraining. Existing methods - such as task arithmetic, model soups, TIES, and DARE - are computationally efficient and empirically successful, but rely on heuristic design choices and lack formal optimality guarantees. We show that merging can be formulated as a convex quadratic programme over residual updates, yielding weights that minimise a squared-output calibration objective using calibration inputs and fine-tuned model outputs, and subsuming existing methods as special cases. Our framework yields a closed-form diagnostic - the fraction of residual energy captured by a chosen basis - that predicts downstream merge quality using only the calibration set. Empirically, the QP matches or outperforms existing methods in the single-layer setting, and we characterise when the optimal basis provides significant gains over the cheaper diagonal QP. We extend to multi-layer merging via a sequential layer-wise algorithm and demonstrate consistent gains across language and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [32] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性探针可以近乎完美地解码表征，却与模型的实际计算完全正交。本文通过日历日期时长推理任务发现，sin/cos探针能恢复一年中的第几天信息，但消融该方向不影响模型输出；而同一层通过分布式对齐搜索找到的四维子空间消融后彻底破坏性能。两者夹角与随机子空间的哈达均匀零分布无区别，表明探针学到了与模型计算正交的方向。电路逆向工程揭示了原因：注意力头通过±30和±61天的QK偏移路由月份粒度上下文，MLP将绝对日期转换为时长。稀疏自编码器分解证实了语义分离。该现象在四种规模和两个模型家族中复现，并在空间位移和符号算术领域有初步证据，表明读出头-中介体正交性是探针可解释性的普遍失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示线性探针尽管能准确解码表征，却可能与模型实际计算过程正交这一关键问题，质疑基于探针的可解释性方法及安全监控方案的有效性。

Method: 在日历日期时长推理任务上，使用sin/cos探针与分布式对齐搜索(DAS)对比；引入读出头-中介体角度度量并与Haar均匀零分布比较；通过电路逆向工程分析注意力头和MLP的机制；使用稀疏自编码器分解特征语义；跨四种模型规模(1.5B-9B)和两个模型家族复现，并扩展到空间位移和符号算术领域。

Result: 探针方向与因果子空间正交，角度与随机子空间无区别；消融探针方向不影响模型性能而消融DAS子空间完全破坏性能；注意力头通过±30和±61天的QK偏移处理月份信息，MLP将绝对日期转换为时长；稀疏自编码器显示探针对齐和DAS对齐的特征编码语义不相交的概念。

Conclusion: 读出头-中介体正交性是探针可解释性的普遍失败模式，直接削弱了将探针用作运行时安全监控器的提案，因为探针可能在模型已静默放弃的方向上报告高置信度。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [33] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了蒸馏和量化作为扩展现有模型家族至新规模和硬件格式的经济高效方法，基于Apertus 8B模型，通过1.7T令牌训练出参数高达4B的Apertus-v1.1蒸馏模型家族，在覆盖广泛硬件和系统需求方面展现了成本效益和强准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛应用于各类场景（如聊天助手、数据标注），需要满足不同的预算和硬件约束。当前趋势是发布一系列不同规模的相似模型以适应广泛约束，但如何经济高效地扩展模型家族至新规模和硬件格式是一个关键问题。

Method: 采用蒸馏（distillation）和量化（quantization）作为经济高效的方法，基于开源配方Apertus 8B LLM，在1.7T许可令牌上训练得到参数规模最高达4B的Apertus-v1.1蒸馏模型家族。

Result: 成功构建了Apertus-v1.1蒸馏模型家族（最高4B参数），在覆盖大规模硬件和系统需求方面展现出成本效率和强大的准确性表现。

Conclusion: 蒸馏和量化是扩展LLM家族至新规模和硬件格式的经济有效途径，能够在不牺牲准确性的前提下满足多样化的硬件和系统约束。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [34] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随机决策理论在线学习中的事件级纯差分隐私问题，提出了一个无时间视界的算法，实现了O(log K/Δ_min + log K/ε)的最优遗憾界，解决了Hu和Mehta在COLT上提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hu和Mehta在COLT会议上提出的开放问题：确定随机决策理论在线学习在事件级纯差分隐私下的最优间隙依赖遗憾率。

Method: 将时间划分为指数增长的数据块，每个数据块内执行单一动作；通过指数机制作用于数据无关的前缀来选取下一动作；利用随机前缀将数据块遗憾转化为softmax选择误差之和；熵势参数控制隐私主导的大间隙动作。

Result: 提出了无时间视界的纯差分隐私算法，证明了显式遗憾界Reg_T ≤ 1000·(log K/Δ_min + log K/ε)，匹配已知下界。

Conclusion: 该成果给出了纯事件级差分隐私下随机决策理论在线学习的最优遗憾率，解决了开放问题。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [35] [RUBRIC-ARROW: Alternating Pointwise Rubric Reward Modeling for LLM Post-training in Non-verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29156)
*Haoxiang Jiang,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Wanying Wang,Ran Xu,Tony Yu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RUBRIC-ARROW框架，通过交替训练评分规则生成器和条件裁判，利用成对偏好数据和概率评分机制解决主观场景下的点式奖励建模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点式奖励建模在主观、不可验证的场景中难以进行绝对评分，现有基于评分规则的方法依赖前沿LLM且存在布尔聚合导致的平局问题。

Method: 提出RUBRIC-ARROW交替训练框架：联合训练评分规则生成器和条件裁判，RL阶段仅使用成对偏好数据；采用基于概率的评分规则减少平局，结合阶段特定偏好奖励和交替GRPO方案训练点式评估器。

Result: 在奖励建模准确率上达到竞争性水平，并为下游策略后训练带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RUBRIC-ARROW通过交替学习和概率评分有效解决了主观场景下的点式奖励建模难题，同时提升了后训练效果。

Abstract: Pointwise reward modeling offers critical signals for LLM post-training, yet struggles with absolute scoring in subjective, non-verifiable settings. Rubric-based methods address this by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, but existing approaches typically depend on frontier LLMs and suffer from ties caused by hard Boolean aggregation. We present RUBRIC-ARROW, an alternating framework that jointly trains a rubric generator and a rubric-conditioned judge, with its RL stage using only pairwise preference data. Our method couples a probability-based scoring rule that reduces ties with phase-specific preference-based rewards and an alternating GRPO scheme that together train the pointwise evaluator. Extensive experiments show that RUBRIC-ARROW achieves competitive reward-modeling accuracy and yields consistent gains for downstream policy post-training.

</details>


### [36] [Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157)
*Yifei Zuo,Dhruv Pai,Zhichen Zeng,Alec Dewulf,Shuming Hu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallax，一种参数化的局部线性注意力机制（Local Linear Attention），通过引入可学习的查询投影器替代数值求解器，并设计硬件感知算法提升计算强度，在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中实现一致的困惑度改进和下游任务迁移优势，首次展示了注意力机制中架构-优化器协同设计的实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统softmax注意力使用局部常数估计，存在偏差-方差权衡次优的问题；局部线性注意力（LLA）虽然理论上更优，但由于计算复杂度和数值稳定性问题难以在大规模LLM预训练中扩展。

Method: 1) 提出Parallax，用可学习的类查询投影器替代LLA中的数值求解器，用于探测KV协方差；2) 将Parallax置于由带宽、探针结构和仿射结构连接的注意力机制统一框架中；3) 设计硬件感知算法，提升算术强度使注意力进入更计算密集的计算边界；4) 使用Muon优化器解锁Parallax能力。

Result: 1) 原型解码核在多种批大小和上下文长度下匹配或超越FlashAttention 2/3；2) 在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中实现一致的困惑度改进，并迁移到下游基准任务；3) 在参数匹配和计算匹配控制下均保持优势，实现帕累托改进；4) 发现Muon优化器能解锁Parallax能力的现象。

Conclusion: Parallax证明了局部线性注意力机制可以在LLM中规模化应用，实现了架构-优化器协同设计的首个实证成果，为注意力机制的改进提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the central paradigm in artificial intelligence, yet the core computational primitive of attention has remained structurally unchanged. Local Linear Attention (LLA) is an attention mechanism derived from nonparametric statistics in the test-time regression framework. In contrast to prior research on efficient attention variants, LLA upgrades the local constant estimate in softmax attention to a local linear estimate, yielding provably superior bias-variance tradeoffs for associative memory. However, LLA has not been scaled in LLM pretraining due to computational and numerical stability concerns. We introduce Parallax, a parameterized Local Linear Attention that is scalable for LLMs. Parallax eliminates the numerical solver in LLA and learns an extra query-like projector that probes the KV covariance. We place Parallax within a family of attention mechanisms connected by the bandwidth, the probe construction and the affine structure. We propose a hardware-aware algorithm that increases the arithmetic intensity over FlashAttention, shifting attention into a more compute bound regime. Our prototype decode kernel matches or outperforms FlashAttention 2/3 across diverse batch sizes and context lengths. We pretrain Parallax at 0.6B and 1.7B scales and find consistent perplexity improvements throughout pretraining with gains that transfer to downstream benchmarks. The advantage persists under both parameter-matched and compute-matched controls, demonstrating a Pareto improvement. We perform careful pretraining ablations and identify a novel phenomenon whereby Muon unlocks the capacity of Parallax. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration of strong architecture-optimizer codesign for attention mechanisms in the architecture research literature.

</details>


### [37] [PROTOCOL: Late Interaction Retrieval for Protein Homolog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29158)
*Gabrielle Cohn,Rohan Gumaste,Minh Hoang,Vihan Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCol通过将蛋白质表示为残基嵌入集合并使用ColBERT风格的晚期交互机制（MaxSim）进行同源检索，在SCOPe超家族和Pfam clan基准测试上优于序列组成、比对方法、池化PLM和训练得到的单向量基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在蛋白质序列相似性弱的"twilight zone"区域，传统比对方法灵敏度不足；已有的基于蛋白质语言模型的检索方法通常将表征池化为单一向量，可能掩盖揭示远缘同源性的局部基序、结构域或保守残基信息。

Method: ProtoCol将蛋白质表示为残基嵌入集合，采用ColBERT风格的晚期交互机制（Late Interaction），独立编码蛋白质并预计算候选表示，通过残基嵌入间的MaxSim操作进行候选打分。

Result: 在SCOPe超家族和Pfam clan基准测试上，ProtoCol优于序列组成方法、比对方法、池化PLM基线以及训练得到的单向量基线方法。

Conclusion: 残基级别的晚期交互机制是一种有效的远缘同源检索方案，能够保留局部信息从而提升检索灵敏度。

Abstract: Protein homology search underlies function annotation, structure prediction, and evolutionary analysis, but remains challenging in the "twilight zone," where global sequence similarity is weak and classical alignment methods lose sensitivity. Protein language models provide context-aware representations that could improve alignment sensitivity in this regime. However, prior protein embedding-based retrieval pipelines often pool these representations into a single vector, potentially obscuring local motifs, domains, or conserved residues that reveal remote homology. We introduce ProtoCol, a model which represents proteins as sets of residue embeddings and uses ColBERT-style late interaction to test whether residue-level comparison improves homolog retrieval. ProtoCol encodes proteins independently, keeps candidate representations pre-computable, and scores candidates with MaxSim over residue embeddings. On SCOPe superfamily and Pfam clan benchmarks, ProtoCol outperforms sequence-composition, alignment-based, pooled PLM, and trained single-vector baselines, supporting late interaction as an effective retrieval layer for remote homology search.

</details>


### [38] [Evolutionary Refinement of Generative Graph Topologies: A Hybrid WGAN-GA Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29161)
*James Sargant,Seyedeh Ava Razi Razavi,Renata Dividino,Sheridan Houghten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用遗传算法（GA）对基于GAN的图生成模型所生成的图进行边级优化，以校正其在度分布和谱分布等结构属性上与真实图的偏差，实验表明该方法能有效降低综合最大均值差异（MMD），生成更逼真的图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于GAN的图生成方法虽然改进了边建模并匹配了类别特定的密度分布，但在度分布和谱分布等关键结构属性上仍与真实图存在显著偏差，需要一种方法来纠正这些残余的结构偏差。

Method: 在GAN图生成框架（生成器产生节点特征和连接模式，GNN判别器评估图真实性和类别一致性）基础上，引入遗传算法（GA）对生成图的边进行精细化调整，引导合成图更接近真实数据分布，同时保持多样性和新颖性。

Result: 实验结果表明，GA精炼能够持续降低综合最大均值差异（MMD），使生成图更接近真实图的结构模式。

Conclusion: 进化精炼是一种有效且灵活的方式，能够纠正基于GAN的图生成器中的残余结构偏差，提高其在真实图合成和数据增强中的适用性。

Abstract: Generating realistic graph-structured data is challenging due to discrete connectivity, varying graph sizes, and class-specific structural patterns. Recent Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN)-based graph generation methods improve edge modelling by learning connectivity and matching class-specific density distributions. However these models still exhibit noticeable deviations such as in degree and spectral distribution when compared to real graphs, indicating that important structural properties are not fully preserved. This work aims to reduce these deviations by refining the graphs produced by an existing GAN-based graph generator framework with a Genetic Algorithm (GA). In the GAN framework, the generator produces both node features and connectivity patterns, while a GNN-based critic evaluates graph realism and class consistency to ensure global structural and class alignment. Building on this foundation, we apply a GA to refine the edges of generated graphs. The refinement process guides synthetic graphs toward closer agreement with real data, while preserving diversity and novelty. Experimental results show that the GA refinement consistently lowers combined Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) compared to the base model, leading to graphs that more closely match real structural patterns. This demonstrates that evolutionary refinement is an effective and flexible way to correct residual structural deviations in GAN-based graph generators, improving their suitability for realistic graph synthesis and data augmentation.

</details>


### [39] [Probabilistic bias adjustment of seasonal forecasts using generative machine learning: A case study of Arctic sea ice predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29172)
*Parsa Gooya,Reinel Sospedra-Alfonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件变分自编码器（cVAE）的改进概率后处理框架，用于生成大规模、高分辨率、偏差校正的季节性海冰预测集成，解决了标准cVAE预测模糊和细尺度能量丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性气候预测对规划和风险管理至关重要，但现有集合预报存在计算成本高、系统性偏差和时空误差等问题。标准cVAE后处理方法虽然能生成校准的预测，但存在预测模糊、缺乏细尺度能量等局限。

Method: 将cVAE中的高斯参数化解码器替换为生成器，在目标函数中用连续排名概率评分（CRPS）替代均方误差（MSE），并使用比原始预报更高分辨率的目标数据集。

Result: 调整后的预报在校准性、与观测分布的一致性、误差大小方面均优于基准预测，同时增强了原始预报的分辨率，改善了预测的锐度（sharpness）和谱功率（spectral power）。

Conclusion: 所提出的改进框架能有效克服标准cVAE的模糊性限制，生成更高质量、高分辨率的偏差校正季节性预测，具有更好的概率校准和空间细节保留能力。

Abstract: Seasonal climate predictions support planning and risk management by offering early information of the most likely-to-occur climate conditions in the coming months, and associated uncertainties. Ensemble forecasts enable this by simulating many plausible outcomes, allowing predictions to be expressed as usable probabilities. Large ensembles and high-resolution forecasts strengthen this guidance by better sampling uncertainty and capturing finer-scale processes but come with significant computational cost. Moreover, forecast ensembles drift and exhibit systematic biases and spatio-temporal errors that grow with lead time, requiring careful post-processing and calibration. A probabilistic post-processing framework based on conditional Variational Autoencoders (cVAEs) was developed at the Canadian Center for Climate Modeling and Analysis to generate large ensembles of bias adjusted seasonal predictions of Arctic sea ice. The generative model was designed to learn the observational distribution conditioned on the biased model prediction. This enables generation of arbitrarily large ensembles of well-calibrated, bias corrected forecasts with improved skill. Here, we extend this framework to address the loss of fine-scale energy and the characteristic blurriness in predictions, a known limitation of standard cVAEs. Specifically, we employ a generator in place of the Gaussian parametrized decoder in the cVAE and use Continuous Ranked Probability Score in the objective function instead of the Mean Square Error. We further use a higher resolution target dataset compared to the raw forecast. We show that the adjusted forecasts are better calibrated, more consistent with the observational distribution, and exhibit smaller errors than benchmark predictions, while also enhancing the resolution of the raw forecasts and improving sharpness and spectral power relative to the standard cVAE.

</details>


### [40] [TIMEGATE: Sustainable Time-Boxed Promotion Gates for Continual ML Adaptation Under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29183)
*Abhijit Chakrabroty,Suddhasvatta Das,Kevin A. Gary,Yash Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIMEGATE是一个策略管理层，通过预算时间、标注、训练和评估来管理持续适应中的ML系统。它发出度量可用性信号M来指导部分与完整评估决策。实验表明：标注比训练效果好2.3倍；在LLaMA-3.1-8B+QLoRA上准确率从0.80提升至0.96（35/36次运行中M=1）；M信号具有信息量；100轮模拟实现66%评估计算节省且无静默错误提升；10%切片评估在单张H200上节省89%时间和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ML系统向持续适应演进，每次重训练循环都需要消耗计算资源、标注资源和能源。现有方法缺乏一个统一的策略层来管理这些资源的分配，特别是在何时进行部分评估与完整评估的决策上。

Method: 提出TIMEGATE策略管理层，通过预算时间、标注、训练和评估四个维度来管理适应过程。核心设计是发出一个度量可用性信号M，用于指导部分评估与完整评估的决策。

Result: (i) 标注在Adult表格数据集上比训练效果好2.3倍；(ii) 在LLaMA-3.1-8B+QLoRA上迁移至SST-2任务，准确率从0.80提升至0.96，35/36次运行中M=1；(iii) M信号信息量丰富，28单元敏感性分析显示在严格阈值下M降至0.81；(iv) 100轮模拟实现66%评估计算节省，且无静默错误提升；(v) LLaMA上的10%切片评估在单张H200上节省89%时间和能耗，比率差异仅0.2%。

Conclusion: TIMEGATE通过度量可用性信号M有效管理持续学习中的资源分配，显著降低计算和能源成本而不损失性能，验证了其在表格数据和大型语言模型上的有效性。

Abstract: As machine learning(ML) systems evolve to continual adaptation, each re-training cycle uses compute, annotation, and energy. We introduce TIMEGATE, a policy layer managing adaptation by budgeting time, labeling, training, and evaluation. TIMEGATE emits a metric-availability signal M for partial vs. full-evaluation decisions. We validate: (i) labeling outperforms training by 2.3x on Adult tabular; (ii) it transfers to LLaMA-3.1-8B + QLoRA on SST-2 (accuracy 0.80 to 0.96; M =1 in 35/36 runs); (iii) M is informative, 28-cell sensitivity shows M drops to 0.81 at tight thresholds; (iv) 100-cycle simulation achieves 66% evaluation-compute savings with no silent mis-promotions; (v) 10%-slice evaluation on LLaMA uses 89% less wall-clock and energy on a single H200 (ratios agree to 0.2%).

</details>


### [41] [Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression: Scientific Discovery via LLM-Driven Equation Search with Granular Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29184)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Samuel Holt,Nabeel Seedat,David L. Bentley,Jim Weatherall,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IGSR的符号回归方法，结合LLM、影响分数和MCTS，通过细粒度术语贡献评估引导方程发现，在多个基准测试中表现优异，并在真实生物数据中发现了经湿实验验证的新关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于符号回归时，通常仅使用全局标量指标（如MSE）作为反馈信号，无法区分方程中哪些项驱动性能、哪些项引入误差，导致搜索效率低下且缺乏可解释性。

Method: IGSR采用两阶段迭代框架：1) LLM生成候选基函数，构建线性模型；2) 通过影响分数Δ_j量化每项对泛化精度的边际贡献，基于此进行剪枝优化。将该机制集成到MCTS中，平衡探索新函数形式与利用高影响成分。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench、药代动力学PKPD模型、流行病学模拟和真实基因组数据等多个基准上验证了有效性。在高维生物数据案例中，发现了DNA甲基化与RNA聚合酶II暂停之间的新关系，并经湿实验验证。

Conclusion: IGSR通过细粒度影响引导机制，显著提升了LLM在符号回归中的搜索效率与发现能力，能够从高维数据中发现可验证的新科学假设，为AI驱动的科学发现提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for scientific discovery, yet their application to symbolic regression is often constrained by inefficient search strategies and coarse feedback signals. Current methods typically guide LLMs using scalar metrics (e.g., global Mean Squared Error), which fail to identify which components of a proposed equation are driving performance or causing error. We introduce \textit{Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression} (IGSR), a method that frames equation discovery as an iterative two-step process combining diverse term generation with rigorous selection: an LLM generates candidate basis functions $ψ_j(\mathbf{x})$ for a linear model, which are then evaluated using granular influence scores $Δ_j$. These scores quantify each term's marginal contribution to generalization accuracy, enabling an influence-guided pruning process that systematically refines the model structure. Integrating this mechanism into a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enables navigating the combinatorial search space while balancing exploration of novel functional forms with exploitation of high-influence components. We demonstrate IGSR's effectiveness on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including LLM-SRBench, pharmacological PKPD models, an epidemiological simulation, and real-world genomic data. Notably, we validate the framework's capacity for genuine discovery in a case study using a high-dimensional biological dataset, in which IGSR identified a novel relationship between DNA methylation and RNA Polymerase II pausing; a hypothesis that was subsequently supported via wet-lab experimentation.

</details>


### [42] [When RL Suppresses Its Own Vocabulary: Recovering Reasoning Diversity in Puzzle-to-Math Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29190)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Arjun Karuvally,Terrence Sejnowski,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)在LLM推理中的跨域迁移问题。通过引入推理原语级分析框架，发现仅使用约束满足谜题进行SFT和RL训练，可以显著提升模型在数学领域的推理能力（OlymMATH-Hard上pass@32从16%提升至36%）。进一步提出新颖性奖励机制，缓解标准RL对探索性原语（如假设、回溯）的抑制，带来额外性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升LLM推理能力，但跨域迁移的条件和机制尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏对推理过程粒度的分析工具，无法解释迁移为何发生。

Method: 1) 提出推理原语级分析框架：9类跨度分类器+动机提取，将思维链分割为原语动机并追踪其演变；2) 仅使用约束满足谜题（无数学题）进行SFT和RL训练（Vanilla GSPO）；3) 提出新颖性奖励（参考模型下的困惑度作为信号），奖励多样化的正确轨迹。

Result: 1) 谜题SFT带来+7pp pass@32提升；2) Vanilla GSPO进一步+6pp；3) 新颖性奖励额外+7pp；4) 端到端方案将OlymMATH-Hard从16.0%提升至36.0%，且SFT/RL阶段均未使用数学数据。

Conclusion: 约束满足谜题训练的推理原语可以跨域迁移到数学推理。标准RL会抑制探索性原语，而新颖性奖励能恢复这些原语并进一步提升性能。该发现为构建无需目标领域数据的通用推理能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning, but the conditions under which it transfers across domains -- and why it does so -- remain under-explored. We study cross-domain transfer in a 7B model whose SFT and RL post-training stages use only constraint-satisfaction puzzles, with no mathematics problems in the post-training data. To analyze how transfer emerges, we introduce a reasoning primitive-level framework that combines a 9-class span classifier with motif extraction, allowing us to segment chain-of-thought traces into primitive motifs and track their evolution across training stages and domains. We find that puzzle SFT induces a reasoning-primitive vocabulary, yielding a $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} gain on OlymMATH-Hard. Vanilla GSPO then composes these primitives into longer compute-verify chains, adding a further $+6$pp. However, this RL stage also suppresses exploratory primitives such as \textit{hypothesize} and \textit{backtrack}. To address this, we introduce a novelty bonus that rewards diverse correct rollouts, using perplexity under the reference model as a signal. This restores recovery primitives during RL and adds a further $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} relative to vanilla GSPO. Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-music generation enable high-fidelity synthesis of structured musical audio, raising growing concerns about data provenance, consent, and training transparency. These models are typically trained on large-scale corpora with little disclosure, leaving no practical mechanism to verify whether a particular audio sample was included in training. In this paper, we investigate black-box membership inference for generative music models, aiming to determine whether a candidate music sample was used during training, given only query access to the deployed system. Our key insight is that training membership induces systematically stronger semantic and structural alignment between a candidate sample and the model's generation conditioned on its caption. We query the target model with the associated caption and measure the relationship between the candidate audio and the generated output in a learned feature space. To capture features that separate members from non-members, we construct paired examples consisting of each track and its caption-conditioned generation from shadow models, and train a music auditor to classify membership. The auditor captures alignment patterns characteristic of training membership and generalizes to unseen target models in a fully black-box setting without access to model parameters or training metadata. Across multiple state-of-the-art music generators, our method achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, with false-positive and false-negative rates as low as 1.9% and 1.0%, demonstrating that reliable training-data auditing is feasible in realistic deployment scenarios.

*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种黑盒方法，仅通过分析模型生成的文本输出，就能推断大语言模型的参数量下限，揭示了闭源模型的设计策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大语言模型的参数量常被开发者隐瞒，而模型规模是理解能力与成本的关键参考指标，现有方法无法在仅访问API的情况下获取这一信息。

Method: 利用广泛流传文本（经典文献、宗教文本等）几乎出现在所有预训练语料中的事实，通过测量模型在不同长度文本片段上的下一词预测准确率，提取记忆化信号，构建准确率分布向量；进而提出两种推断方法：成对统计检验（判断两个模型的大小关系）和基于主成分分析的标度律估计器（将信号映射到参数量）。

Result: 在开源模型上验证了方法的准确性和可靠性；应用于闭源模型时，成功恢复其内部产品层级，并发掘出业界不同的扩展策略——部分开发者追求代际参数增长，另一些则受限于参数上限。

Conclusion: 即使仅在严格的API限制下，也能系统性地探知闭源模型隐藏的设计选择与扩展策略。

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>


### [45] [BlockBatch: Multi-Scale Consensus Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29233)
*Xiaoyou Wu,Cheng-Jhih Shih,Binfei Ji,Yong Liu,Yingyan,Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BlockBatch，一种无需训练的在线推理框架，通过并行执行多个块大小的分支（利用置信度门控令牌合并、领导者同步和周期性全局刷新）来加速扩散语言模型推理，在保持准确率的同时平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE并实现1.33倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在块级推理中存在粒度权衡困境：小块保持局部条件性但需要更多去噪步，大块并行性更强但可能过早承诺并累积缓存误差。现有加速方法通常为每个请求选择单一块大小，未能利用不同块大小之间的互补性。

Method: 提出BlockBatch框架，包含三个关键组件：1) 置信度门控令牌合并，在不同分支间协调生成结果；2) 领导者同步机制，确保分支间一致性；3) 周期性全序列刷新，将局部块更新重新锚定到全局一致的KV状态。

Result: 在3种代表性dLLM和4个数据集上，BlockBatch平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE，实现1.33倍端到端加速（对比Fast-dLLM），同时保持准确率。

Conclusion: 块大小多样性是分支并行dLLM推理中一个实用且此前未被充分探索的优化维度。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising multiple token positions in parallel, offering an attractive alternative to strictly autoregressive decoding. In practice, however, block-wise dLLM inference exposes a difficult granularity trade-off: small blocks preserve local conditioning but require many denoising steps, whereas large blocks expose more parallelism but can make premature commitments and accumulate cache error. Existing acceleration methods typically choose a single block size per request, leaving the complementarity among block sizes unused. We show that block size itself is a useful branching dimension. Different block sizes induce related but non-identical KV-cache trajectories: branches often share an initial prefix, bifurcate at semantically decisive positions, and later agree on syntactically lightweight tokens. Motivated by this structure, we propose BlockBatch, a training-free online inference framework that executes multiple block-size branches for the same request inside a batched forward pass. BlockBatch coordinates these branches through confidence-gated token merging, leader-based synchronization, and periodic full-sequence refreshes that re-anchor local block updates to a globally consistent KV state. Across 3 representative dLLMs and 4 datasets, BlockBatch reduces denoising NFEs by 26.6\% on average and achieves a 1.33$\times$ average end-to-end speedup over Fast-dLLM while preserving accuracy. These results identify block-size diversity as a practical and previously underexplored axis for branch-parallel dLLM inference.

</details>


### [46] [KLAS: Using Similarity to Stitch Neural Networks for Improved Accuracy-Efficiency Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29259)
*Debopam Sanyal,Anantharaman Iyer,Alind Khare,Trisha Jain,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Clayton Kerce,Alexey Tumanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KLAS框架，通过KL散度度量模型间中间表示的相似性，自动化选择最优拼接配置，从而在固定计算预算下获得更好的准确率-效率权衡曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拼接方法依赖启发式规则选择拼接配置，导致次优的准确率-效率权衡且缺乏泛化性；需要一种能够显式捕捉预训练模型间相似性的自动化拼接选择方法。

Method: 提出KLAS框架，利用KL散度衡量预训练模型中间层表示的相似性，从O(k²n²)种可能的拼接配置中自动选择最优的二进制拼接方案，实现跨模型家族的泛化。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，KLAS在相同计算成本下最高提升1.21% top-1准确率，或在保持准确率的同时减少1.33倍FLOPs。

Conclusion: KLAS通过显式建模模型间相似性，实现了更优的准确率-效率权衡，且自动化程度更高、泛化性更强，优于现有启发式拼接方法。

Abstract: Given the wide range of deployment targets, flexible model selection is essential for optimizing performance within a given compute budget. Recent work demonstrates that stitching pretrained models within a model family enables cost-effective interpolation of the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff space. Stitching transforms intermediate activations from one pretrained model into another, producing a new interpolated stitched network. Such networks provide a pool of deployment options along the accuracy-efficiency spectrum. However, existing stitching approaches often yield suboptimal tradeoffs and lack generalizability, as they primarily rely on heuristics to select stitch configurations. We argue that constructing improved accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs requires explicitly capturing and leveraging the similarity between pretrained models being stitched. To this end, we introduce KLAS, a novel stitch selection framework that automates and generalizes stitch selection across model families by leveraging KL divergence between intermediate representations. KLAS identifies the most promising binary stitches from the $O(k^2n^2)$ possibilities for $k$ pretrained models of depth $n$. Through comprehensive experiments, we demonstrate that KLAS improves the accuracy-efficiency curve of stitched models at the same finetuning cost as baselines. KLAS achieves up to $1.21\%$ higher ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy at the same computational cost, or maintains accuracy with a $1.33\times$ reduction in FLOPs.

</details>

*Sakshi Kumari,Shyam Kumar M,Sushmitha P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Adam和AMSGrad等自适应优化器，并提出了基于视线方法的新优化器C-Adam，提供了收敛性理论证明并通过数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化器（如Adam）虽然广泛应用，但存在不保证收敛的问题；AMSGrad虽解决了非收敛问题，但仍有改进空间。本文旨在提出一种能更好平衡计算成本与振荡问题的新优化器。

Method: 基于视线方法（line of sight approach）提出C-Adam优化器，并在理论层面给出收敛性证明，同时在多个真实数值实验中进行验证。
